Use as step 1 of $c360-usecase. ([sairajsawant/snowflake-c360](https://github.com/sairajsawant/snowflake-c360/blob/HEAD/.cortex/agents/c360-scout.md))

- **ca-debugger**: Runs a scientific-method debugging session, maintaining .code_assist/.debug/DEBUG.md across the investigation.
